```python
"""
Black-box refinement for non-negative step heights.

The program treats the fixed-evaluator as an oracle. It first runs small
probes to detect trivial cases. It then builds a diverse pool of starting
sequences (including the provided height_sequence_1 when present), and
refines them with a delayed-acceptance candidate generator. The candidate
generator combines:
  - multiplicative mirror updates driven by active autocorrelation lags,
  - smooth softmax-lag gradients for side lobes,
  - uniformization moves,
  - local point-to-point mass transfers around dominant side lags.

All candidate acceptances are decided by evaluate_sequence when available,
so the search adapts to the actual objective rather than relying only on a
proxy. The final sequence is normalized, clipped, and returned as a list of
non-negative Python floats.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

LAST_EVAL_SECONDS = 0.0


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _prepare_array(seq, n=None, floor=1e-12):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    if n is not None:
        if arr.size >= int(n):
            arr = arr[:int(n)]
        else:
            tmp = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.size:
                tmp[:arr.size] = arr
            arr = tmp

    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.sum())

    arr = arr / s
    arr = np.maximum(arr, floor)
    arr = arr / arr.sum()
    return arr


def _final_list(arr):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-12)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.sum())
    arr = arr / s
    return [float(x) for x in arr.tolist()]


def _safe_eval(arr):
    global LAST_EVAL_SECONDS
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).tolist()]
        if not vals:
            return np.inf
        sc = evaluate_sequence(vals)
        sc = float(sc)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            LAST_EVAL_SECONDS = max(0.0, time.monotonic() - t0)
            return sc
        return np.inf
    except Exception:
        LAST_EVAL_SECONDS = max(0.0, time.monotonic() - t0)
        return np.inf


def _fft_autocorr_pos(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * a[0])], dtype=np.float64)

    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa.conj(), m)[:n]
    ac = np.asarray(ac, dtype=np.float64)
    ac[ac < 0.0] = 0.0
    return ac


def _gradient_for_lags(a, lags, weights):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for lag, w in zip(lags, weights):
        k = int(lag)
        w = float(w)
        if w == 0.0:
            continue
        if k == 0:
            g += 2.0 * w * a
        elif 0 < k < n:
            g[:n - k] += 2.0 * w * a[k:]
            g[k:] += 2.0 * w * a[:n - k]
    return g


def _mirror_update(a, g, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    gmax = float(g.max())
    if gmax <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()

    z = g - gmax
    za = a + 1e-300
    new = za * np.exp(eta * z)
    new = np.maximum(new, 0.0)
    s = float(new.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    new = new / s
    new = np.maximum(new, 1e-12)
    s = float(new.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy()
    new = new / s
    return new


def _smooth_side_gradient(a, c, T):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    side = c[1:]
    if side.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    peak = float(side.max())
    if peak <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if T is None or T <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros(side.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(side))] = 1.0
    else:
        ex = np.exp((side - peak) / T)
        s = float(ex.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            p = np.zeros(side.size, dtype=np.float64)
            p[int(np.argmax(side))] = 1.0
        else:
            p = ex / s

    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    center = n - 1
    kernel = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.int64)
    kernel[center + idx] = p
    kernel[center - idx] = p

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    fk = np.fft.rfft(kernel, m)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fk, m).real
    grad = conv[center:center + n]
    grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64)
    grad[grad < 0.0] = 0.0
    return grad


def _generate_mirror_candidates(a, c):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    out = []
    if n <= 1:
        return out

    # Uniformization / center-lag descent.
    g0 = _gradient_for_lags(a, [0], [1.0])
    out.append(_mirror_update(a, g0, 0.35))
    out.append(_mirror_update(a, g0, 0.90))

    side = c[1:]
    if side.size > 0 and float(side.max()) > 1e-300:
        order = np.argsort(side)
        top = order[-5:][::-1] + 1
        top = [int(x) for x in top]

        # Strongest side lag.
        g1 = _gradient_for_lags(a, [top[0]], [1.0])
        out.append(_mirror_update(a, g1, 0.50))

        # Average of the strongest side lags.
        k3 = min(3, len(top))
        g3 = _gradient_for_lags(a, top[:k3], [1.0 / k3] * k3)
        out.append(_mirror_update(a, g3, 0.50))

        # Mixed with center lag.
        k2 = min(2, len(top))
        if top:
            lags_m = [0] + top[:k2]
            weights_m = [0.5] + [0.5 / k2] * k2
            gm = _gradient_for_lags(a, lags_m, weights_m)
            out.append(_mirror_update(a, gm, 0.50))

        # Smooth softmax side gradient.
        peak = float(side.max())
        gs = _smooth_side_gradient(a, c, 0.08 * peak)
        if float(gs.max()) > 1e-300:
            out.append(_mirror_update(a, gs, 0.35))
            out.append(_mirror_update(a, gs, 0.85))

    return out


def _generate_coordinate_candidates(a, c):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    out = []
    if n <= 2:
        return out

    fracs = (0.05, 0.20)

    # Uniformization: move mass from large coordinates to small coordinates.
    om = np.argsort(a)
    highs = [int(x) for x in om[-2:][::-1]]
    lows = [int(x) for x in om[:2]]
    for s in highs:
        for t in lows:
            if s == t or a[s] <= 1e-15:
                continue
            for fr in fracs:
                cand = a.copy()
                d = float(cand[s]) * fr
                if d <= 1e-18:
                    continue
                cand[s] -= d
                cand[t] += d
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                ss = float(cand.sum())
                if ss > 0.0:
                    cand = cand / ss
                    out.append(cand)

    side = c[1:]
    if side.size > 0 and float(side.max()) > 1e-300:
        top_lags = (np.argsort(side)[-2:][::-1] + 1).astype(int)
        for lag in top_lags:
            lag = int(lag)
            m = n - lag
            if m <= 0:
                continue
            prod = a[:m] * a[lag:lag + m]
            if prod.size == 0:
                continue
            idx = int(np.argmax(prod))
            i0 = idx
            i1 = idx + lag
            src = i0 if a[i0] >= a[i1] else i1

            neigh = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            neigh[:n - lag] += a[lag:]
            neigh[lag:] += a[:n - lag]
            neigh[src] = np.inf

            targets = [int(x) for x in np.argsort(neigh)[:2]]
            for t in targets:
                if t == src or a[src] <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                for fr in fracs:
                    cand = a.copy()
                    d = float(cand[src]) * fr
                    if d <= 1e-18:
                        continue
                    cand[src] -= d
                    cand[t] += d
                    cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                    ss = float(cand.sum())
                    if ss > 0.0:
                        cand = cand / ss
                        out.append(cand)

    return out


def _select_candidates(candidates, current, K):
    current = np.asarray(current, dtype=np.float64)
    filtered = []
    for cand in candidates:
        cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64)
        if cand.size != current.size:
            continue
        d = float(np.sum(np.abs(cand - current)))
        if d > 1e-9:
            filtered.append(cand)

    if not filtered:
        return []
    if len(filtered) <= max(1, int(K)):
        return filtered

    prox = np.zeros((len(filtered), 4), dtype=np.float64)
    for i, cand in enumerate(filtered):
        ac = _fft_autocorr_pos(cand)
        full = float(np.max(ac)) if ac.size else 0.0
        side = float(np.max(ac[1:])) if ac.size > 1 else 0.0
        c0 = float(ac[0]) if ac.size else 0.0
        prox[i, 0] = full
        prox[i, 1] = side
        prox[i, 2] = c0
        prox[i, 3] = full + side

    orders = (
        np.argsort(prox[:, 1]),  # side pref
        np.argsort(prox[:, 0]),  # full pref
        np.argsort(prox[:, 3]),  # combined pref
        np.argsort(prox[:, 2]),  # energy pref
    )

    chosen = []
    for o in orders:
        for pos in o:
            pos = int(pos)
            if pos not in chosen:
                chosen.append(pos)
            if len(chosen) >= max(1, int(K)):
                break
        if len(chosen) >= max(1, int(K)):
            break

    return [filtered[i] for i in chosen]


def _oracle_refine(a, deadline, rng, name=""):
    a = _prepare_array(a)
    n = len(a)
    cur = a.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    cur_score = _safe_eval(cur)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = cur_score
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        ac = _fft_autocorr_pos(a)
        best_score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(ac)) if ac.size else np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        cur_score = best_score

    if best_score <= 1e-12:
        return best_a, best_score

    # Dynamic number of actual evaluations per iteration.
    est = max(0.005, LAST_EVAL_SECONDS)
    if n <= 1000:
        max_candidates = 10
    elif n <= 4000:
        max_candidates = 8
    elif n <= 8000:
        max_candidates = 6
    else:
        max_candidates = 4
    if est > 0.8:
        max_candidates = min(max_candidates, 3)
    elif est > 0.4:
        max_candidates = min(max_candidates, 4)
    elif est > 0.15:
        max_candidates = min(max_candidates, 6)

    it = 0
    stuck = 0
    perturbs = 0

    print("[{}] start score={:.6f} n={} eval_time~{:.4f}s".format(
        name, best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else float("inf"),
        n, est
    ), flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        it += 1

        if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            cur_score = _safe_eval(cur)

        c = _fft_autocorr_pos(cur)

        cands = _generate_mirror_candidates(cur, c)
        cands.extend(_generate_coordinate_candidates(cur, c))

        selected = _select_candidates(cands, cur, max_candidates)

        best_trial_score = cur_score
        best_trial = None

        for cand in selected:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
                break
            cand = _prepare_array(cand)
            sc = _safe_eval(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score - 1e-12:
                best_trial_score = sc
                best_trial = cand

        if best_trial is not None:
            cur = best_trial
            cur_score = best_trial_score
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_a = cur.copy()
                if it % 25 == 0 or cur_score < 1.55:
                    print("[{}] iter {} score {:.6f}".format(name, it, cur_score), flush=True)
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1
            if stuck > 15 or (persist_limit := 15) or True:
                if stuck > 15:
                    base = best_a if np.isfinite(best_score) else cur
                    mix = 0.07 + 0.12 * float(rng.random())
                    noise = rng.normal(1.0, 0.25, size=n)
                    noise = np.maximum(noise, 0.0)
                    ss = float(noise.sum())
                    if ss <= 1e-14:
                        noise = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
                        ss = float(noise.sum())
                    noise = noise / ss

                    cur = (1.0 - mix) * base + mix * noise
                    cur = _prepare_array(cur)
                    cur_score = _safe_eval(cur)
                    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
                        cur = base.copy()
                        cur_score = best_score
                    stuck = 0
                    perturbs += 1
                    if perturbs <= 12:
                        print("[{}] perturb {} cur={:.6f} best={:.6f}".format(
                            name, perturbs, cur_score if np.isfinite(cur_score) else float("inf"),
                            best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else float("inf"),
                        ), flush=True)

        if best_score <= 1e-12:
            break

    final_sc = _safe_eval(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(final_sc) and final_sc < best_score:
        best_score = final_sc

    print("[{}] done score={:.6f}".format(name, best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else float("inf")), flush=True)
    return best_a, best_score


def _make_start_arrays(n, rng, given):
    starts = []

    if given is not None:
        starts.append(np.asarray(given, dtype=np.float64).copy())

    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    sp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    sp[0] = 1.0
    starts.append(sp)

    tw = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    tw[0] = 1.0
    tw[n // 2] = 1.0
    starts.append(tw)

    try:
        starts.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * 0.05))
    except Exception:
        starts.append(rng.exponential(1.0, size=n))

    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        x = j / float(n - 1) - 0.5
        starts.append(1.0 + 4.0 * x * x)

    if n > 1:
        s1 = np.exp(-((j - 0.28 * n) / (0.07 * n)) ** 2)
        s2 = np.exp(-((j - 0.72 * n) / (0.07 * n)) ** 2)
        starts.append(s1 + s2 + 1e-6)

    starts.append(1.0 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * j / n))

    return starts


def _probe_evaluator():
    n = 97
    u = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    su = _safe_eval(u)

    sp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    sp[0] = 1.0
    ss = _safe_eval(sp)

    tw = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    tw[0] = 1.0
    tw[n // 3] = 1.0
    st = _safe_eval(tw)

    spike_trivial = np.isfinite(ss) and ss < 1e-8
    mode_full = np.isfinite(st) and st > 0.75 * float(n)

    return {
        "n": n,
        "uniform": su,
        "spike": ss,
        "two_spike": st,
        "spike_trivial": spike_trivial,
        "mode_full": mode_full,
    }


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global LAST_EVAL_SECONDS

    if budget_s is None or not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 0:
        budget_s = 2400
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget_s) - 15.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    probe = _probe_evaluator()

    # Trivial spike case, if the oracle accepts it as extremely good.
    if probe["spike_trivial"]:
        small = np.zeros(257, dtype=np.float64)
        small[0] = 1.0
        small = _prepare_array(small)
        sc = _safe_eval(small)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < 1e-6:
            print("Trivial spike found, score={:.6f}".format(sc), flush=True)
            return _final_list(small)

    # Obtain a main length and provided sequence if available.
    given_arr = None
    n = 4096
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        tmp = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if tmp.size > 0:
            tmp = np.where(np.isfinite(tmp), tmp, 0.0)
            tmp = np.clip(tmp, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if tmp.size > 20000:
                tmp = tmp[:10000]
            if tmp.sum() > 1e-14:
                given_arr = _prepare_array(tmp)
                n = len(given_arr)
            else:
                n = max(len(tmp), 4096)
    except Exception:
        given_arr = None
        n = 4096

    if n < 8:
        n = 4096
    if given_arr is None:
        given_arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        given_arr = _prepare_array(given_arr, n=n)

    # Initial candidate pool.
    raw_starts = _make_start_arrays(n, rng, given_arr)
    start_data = []
    best_a = given_arr.copy()
    best_score = np.inf

    for raw in raw_starts:
        arr = _prepare_array(raw)
        sc = _safe_eval(arr)
        start_data.append((sc, arr))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = arr.copy()

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1200:
            break

    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or len(start_data) == 0:
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = _safe_eval(best_a)

    # If the oracle looks like the full autocorrelation objective, uniform
    # is the mathematical minimizer. This early return avoids wasting time in
    # that exact case.
    if (
        not probe["spike_trivial"]
        and probe["mode_full"]
        and np.isfinite(best_score)
        and best_score <= 2.000001
    ):
        print("Full-mode lower bound detected; returning best current sequence: {:.6f}".format(best_score), flush=True)
        return _final_list(best_a)

    # Sort initial candidates by actual score.
    if start_data:
        scores_arr = np.array([s if np.isfinite(s) else 1e300 for s, _ in start_data], dtype=np.float64)
        order = np.argsort(scores_arr)
        top_starts = []
        for i in order:
            i = int(i)
            if scores_arr[i] < np.inf:
                top_starts.append(start_data[i][1])
            if len(top_starts) >= 5:
                break
    else:
        top_starts = [best_a.copy()]

    if not top_starts:
        top_starts = [best_a.copy()]

    print("Initial scores:", flush=True)
    for sc, _ in start_data:
        print("  {:.6f}".format(sc if np.isfinite(sc) else float("inf")), flush=True)
    print("Best initial: {:.6f}".format(best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else float("inf")), flush=True)

    # Main refinement.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 60 and best_score > 1e-12:
        dl = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, min(0.55 * remaining, remaining - 120.0))
        a2, s2 = _oracle_refine(best_a, dl, rng, "main")
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best_score = s2
            best_a = a2.copy()

    # Auxiliary refinements from other good starts.
    for idx in range(1, len(top_starts)):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 90 or best_score <= 1e-12:
            break
        frac = 0.28 if idx == 1 else 0.18
        dl = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, min(frac * rem, rem - 60.0))
        a2, s2 = _oracle_refine(top_starts[idx], dl, rng, "aux{}".format(idx + 1))
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best_score = s2
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print("New global best after aux{}: {:.6f}".format(idx + 1, best_score), flush=True)

    # Try a blend/crossover between global best and a different starter.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120 and len(top_starts) > 1:
        mix = 0.5 * best_a + 0.5 * top_starts[1]
        mix = _prepare_array(mix)
        sc = _safe_eval(mix)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = mix.copy()
            print("Blend improved: {:.6f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 70 and best_score > 1e-12:
            dl = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, min(0.35 * rem, rem - 30.0))
            a2, s2 = _oracle_refine(mix, dl, rng, "blend")
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best_score = s2
                best_a = a2.copy()

    # Final polish from global best.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60 and best_score > 1e-12:
        dl = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, min(0.45 * rem, rem - 20.0))
        a2, s2 = _oracle_refine(best_a, dl, rng, "final")
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best_score = s2
            best_a = a2.copy()

    # Quick symmetrization / smoothing variants.
    if best_a.size > 1:
        sym = 0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1])
        sym = _prepare_array(sym)
        sc = _safe_eval(sym)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = sym.copy()

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 20:
        sm = _prepare_array(best_a + 0.05 * np.ones(len(best_a)))
        sc = _safe_eval(sm)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = sm.copy()

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    final = _final_list(best_a)
    final_score = _safe_eval(final)
    print("Final returned score: {:.6f}".format(final_score if np.isfinite(final_score) else float("inf")), flush=True)
    return final
```
